The app automatically surfaces the sport that is in season, and you can switch between sports anytime from the sport picker.</div></section></div><hr class="shrink-0 bg-divider border-none w-full h-divider" role="separator"/><div data-slot="base" class="px-0"><h3 data-slot="heading"><button data-slot="trigger" class="flex w-full h-full gap-3 items-center tap-highlight-transparent outline-solid outline-transparent data-[focus-visible=true]:z-10 data-[focus-visible=true]:outline-2 data-[focus-visible=true]:outline-focus data-[focus-visible=true]:outline-offset-2 group px-0 sm:px-6 py-6 flex-row-reverse rounded-xl transition-colors hover:bg-default-100/70 dark:hover:bg-white/[0.04]" type="button" tabindex="0" data-react-aria-pressable="true" id="_R_2fb2npfgqp9flb_" aria-expanded="false"><div class="flex-1 flex flex-col text-start"><span data-slot="title" class="text-foreground text-medium font-medium">What are Agent Picks?</span></div><span aria-hidden="true" data-slot="indicator" class="text-default-400 transition-transform rotate-0 data-[open=true]:-rotate-90 rtl:-rotate-180 rtl:data-[open=true]:-rotate-90"><span></span></span></button></h3><section style="will-change:auto;opacity:0;height:0px;overflow-y:hidden"><div data-slot="content" class="py-2 pt-0 pb-6 px-0 sm:px-6 text-base" id="_R_2fb2npfgqp9flbH1_" role="region" aria-labelledby="_R_2fb2npfgqp9flb_">Agent Picks are the day&#x27;s highest-conviction player props, chosen by an LLM agent that runs every Stat Pick research signal — stats reports, AI analysis, matchup data, and injury ripple effects — through a review pass. A small free preview is shown on the homepage; the full slate of Agent Picks is a Premium feature, with results tracked publicly so you can see how the agent is performing.</div></section></div><hr class="shrink-0 bg-divider border-none w-full h-divider" role="separator"/><div data-slot="base" class="px-0"><h3 data-slot="heading"><button data-slot="trigger" class="flex w-full h-full gap-3 items-center tap-highlight-transparent outline-solid outline-transparent data-[focus-visible=true]:z-10 data-[focus-visible=true]:outline-2 data-[focus-visible=true]:outline-focus data-[focus-visible=true]:outline-offset-2 group px-0 sm:px-6 py-6 flex-row-reverse rounded-xl transition-colors hover:bg-default-100/70 dark:hover:bg-white/[0.04]" type="button" tabindex="0" data-react-aria-pressable="true" id="_R_2jb2npfgqp9flb_" aria-expanded="false"><div class="flex-1 flex flex-col text-start"><span data-slot="title" class="text-foreground text-medium font-medium">How much does it cost?</span></div><span aria-hidden="true" data-slot="indicator" class="text-default-400 transition-transform rotate-0 data-[open=true]:-rotate-90 rtl:-rotate-180 rtl:data-[open=true]:-rotate-90"><span></span></span></button></h3><section style="will-change:auto;opacity:0;height:0px;overflow-y:hidden"><div data-slot="content" class="py-2 pt-0 pb-6 px-0 sm:px-6 text-base" id="_R_2jb2npfgqp9flbH1_" role="region" aria-labelledby="_R_2jb2npfgqp9flb_">Free accounts can view stats for up to 12 players per day (switching between stats on the same player is free), track up to 15 picks per day, and see a preview of Agent Picks. Premium starts at $19.95/month and unlocks unlimited stats, unlimited daily picks, monthly AI analysis credits, the full Agent Picks slate, Stat Picks, the Activity feed, and Injury Ripple Effects. Higher tiers (Gold, Platinum, Diamond) add more AI credits, with Diamond offering unlimited credits. AI analysis costs 1 credit per analysis.</div></section></div><hr class="shrink-0 bg-divider border-none w-full h-divider" role="separator"/><div data-slot="base" class="px-0"><h3 data-slot="heading"><button data-slot="trigger" class="flex w-full h-full gap-3 items-center tap-highlight-transparent outline-solid outline-transparent data-[focus-visible=true]:z-10 data-[focus-visible=true]:outline-2 data-[focus-visible=true]:outline-focus data-[focus-visible=true]:outline-offset-2 group px-0 sm:px-6 py-6 flex-row-reverse rounded-xl transition-colors hover:bg-default-100/70 dark:hover:bg-white/[0.04]" type="button" tabindex="0" data-react-aria-pressable="true" id="_R_2nb2npfgqp9flb_" aria-expanded="false"><div class="flex-1 flex flex-col text-start"><span data-slot="title" class="text-foreground text-medium font-medium">What stats do you provide?</span></div><span aria-hidden="true" data-slot="indicator" class="text-default-400 transition-transform rotate-0 data-[open=true]:-rotate-90 rtl:-rotate-180 rtl:data-[open=true]:-rotate-90"><span></span></span></button></h3><section style="will-change:auto;opacity:0;height:0px;overflow-y:hidden"><div data-slot="content" class="py-2 pt-0 pb-6 px-0 sm:px-6 text-base" id="_R_2nb2npfgqp9flbH1_" role="region" aria-labelledby="_R_2nb2npfgqp9flb_">We provide a range of stats that capture a player&#x27;s recent and potential performance — from basic numbers like recent totals and matchup history vs a specific team, to advanced splits like rest, home/away, positional defense for the NBA, and pitcher arsenal, expected stats, and quality of contact for MLB.
